Got it.
Now I’ll respond strictly as a Senior Software Architect, not as a coder.
Your goal is zero orphan features, zero broken connections, zero security gaps, and fully sequential development for a Pandit Listing Website using Django + Bootstrap + MySQL.
Below is the ARCHITECTURALLY CORRECT, SEQUENTIAL MODULE BREAKDOWN.
If you follow this order, nothing will feel hacked together later.
________________


🧠 CORE PRINCIPLE (READ THIS FIRST)
A module is allowed only if:
* It has a clear owner
* It connects to previous modules
* It does NOT assume future modules
* It does NOT expose insecure paths
* It can run independently when completed
We build from foundation → trust → functionality → discovery → growth.
________________


🧩 SEQUENTIAL MODULE ARCHITECTURE
(Pandit Listing Website)
________________


🟦 MODULE 0 — SYSTEM FOUNDATION (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
Purpose
Create a stable, secure base so future modules don’t break.
Includes
* Django project setup
* MySQL configuration
* Environment separation
* Static & media handling
* Base templates
Dependencies
❌ Depends on nothing
✅ Required by everything
Why first?
If this is weak → every feature becomes fragile.
________________


🟦 MODULE 1 — USER IDENTITY & ROLE SYSTEM (CORE)
Purpose
Every future feature depends on who the user is.
Roles
* Guest
* Pandit (Pending / Approved)
* Admin
Includes
* Custom User model (or extended)
* Role field
* Status field
* Secure login / logout
* Session handling
Dependencies
✔ Module 0
Why now?
No feature exists without identity
________________


🟦 MODULE 2 — PANDIT ONBOARDING (CONTROLLED ENTRY)
Purpose
Allow Pandits to exist in the system without trusting them yet.
Includes
* Pandit profile model (basic)
* Signup via referral link
* Status = pending
* Admin approval required
* No public visibility yet
Dependencies
✔ Module 1
Architectural Rule
Pandit ≠ Visible Pandit
________________

Includes
* Referral link generation
* referred_by relationship

* Public trust display (later)
Dependencies
✔ Module 2

Trust must exist before discovery, not after.

Pandit Moderation, Reporting, Blocking
Below is a clean, complete, and FINAL SUMMARY, converted into clear, sequential tasks, including every decision, modification, and clarification we made in this discussion.

🧠 FINAL SUMMARY
Pandit Moderation, Reporting, Blocking & Appeal System
(Governance + Trust + Safety Layer)
This system ensures:
   * Platform trust
   * Fair moderation
   * No spam or fake pandits
   * Transparency
   * Zero additional cost
   * Backend-enforced security
________________


🔒 CORE PRINCIPLES (LOCKED)
   1. No frontend-only security
   * All decisions enforced in Django backend
   * UI only reflects backend state
   2. No deletion of Pandits
   * Pandits are never deleted
   * Only their visibility and permissions change
   3. Fairness & Due Process
   * Every blocked Pandit gets:
   * Reason for blocking
   * Right to appeal
   * Limited dashboard access
   4. Authority Hierarchy
   * Admin Pandit → limited powers
   * Super Admin → final authority
________________


👥 WHO CAN REPORT A PANDIT (LOCKED)
✅ Registered End Users
✅ Pandits (including Admin Pandits)
❌ Anonymous users
Reason:
   * Prevent spam
   * Ensure accountability
________________


🚩 REPORTING & AUTO-REVIEW (LOCKED)
What “Auto-Review” Means
   * ❌ NOT auto-block
   * ❌ NOT auto-ban
   * ✅ Automatic flag for admin review
Auto-review behavior:
   1. Reports are stored
   2. System counts active reports
   3. When threshold is reached (example: 3):
   * Pandit marked “Under Review”
   * Admin Pandits + Super Admin notified
   4. Humans decide next action
✔ Alerting, not punishment
✔ Safe and professional
________________


🟥 BLOCK TYPES (FINAL & LOCKED)
🔸 1. LIMITED BLOCK
(Triggered by Admin Pandit or Auto-review)
Causes:
   * Multiple reports
   * Rule violations
   * Suspicious behavior
Effects:
   * ⚠ Visible in search with warning badge (“Under Review”)
   * ❌ No new bookings
   * ❌ No referrals
   * ❌ No profile editing
   * ✅ Login allowed
   * ✅ Dashboard access (appeal-only)
   * ✅ Appeal allowed
   * ✅ Notifications sent to:
   * Blocked Pandit
   * Blocking Admin
   * Super Admin
________________


🔸 2. FULL BLOCK
(Triggered ONLY by Super Admin)
Causes:
   * Confirmed fake pandit
   * Serious violations
   * Escalated cases
Effects:
   * ❌ Completely hidden from search
   * ❌ Profile inaccessible
   * ❌ Bookings disabled
   * ❌ Reviews disabled
   * ❌ Referral chain frozen
   * ✅ Login allowed
   * ✅ Limited dashboard (appeal-only)
   * ✅ Appeal allowed
   * ✅ Notifications sent
________________


🔁 APPEAL SYSTEM (MODIFIED & LOCKED)
Why change was made:
Originally dashboard access was blocked.
We corrected this to allow fairness.
Appeal Rules:
   * ✅ Blocked Pandit can always appeal
   * ✅ Appeal visible in Pandit dashboard
   * ✅ Only Super Admin can review appeals
   * ✅ Appeal outcomes:
   * Approved → block removed
   * Rejected → block remains
   * ❌ No multiple active appeals at same time
________________


🔍 SEARCH VISIBILITY RULES (LOCKED)
Backend-enforced logic:
IF status != active → hide
IF full_block → hide completely
IF limited_block → show with warning badge
ELSE → show normally


Frontend does NOT decide this.
________________


🔔 NOTIFICATIONS (NO COST)
Implemented via:
   * Database table
   * Django messages
   * Optional email (console backend in dev)
Triggered on:
   * Report submitted
   * Auto-review triggered
   * Block applied
   * Appeal submitted
   * Appeal resolved
________________


🗄️ DATA MODELS (CONCEPTUAL – LATER MODULE)
1️⃣ PanditReport
   * reporter (user/pandit)
   * pandit
   * reason
   * created_at
   * active
2️⃣ PanditBlock
   * pandit
   * blocked_by
   * block_type (LIMITED / FULL)
   * reason
   * created_at
   * active
3️⃣ PanditAppeal
   * pandit
   * block
   * appeal_text
   * status (submitted / approved / rejected)
   * reviewed_by (Super Admin)
   * created_at
4️⃣ Notification
   * recipient
   * message
   * type
   * read_status
   * created_at
________________

📍 SCOPE (LOCKED)
✅ This task WILL:
   * Validate referral token + referral code during Pandit registration
   * Ensure:
   * Referral link exists
   * Referral is active
   * Usage count < max uses
   * On success:
   * Increment used_count
   * Create ReferralUsage record
   * Set User.referred_by

   * Block registration with clear error
❌ This task will NOT:
   * Approve Pandits
   * Auto-login users

   * Create UI forms

________________


🧠 VALIDATION RULES (FINAL & LOCKED)
A referral is valid only if:
   * Token exists
   * Referral code matches
   * is_active = True
   * used_count < max_uses
   * Referrer is NOT fully blocked
If any check fails → ❌ registration blocked.
________________


🟢 CURSOR AI PROMPT
(Copy–paste EXACTLY as-is into Cursor)
You are acting as a senior Django backend developer.


Task 3.4: Validate referral during Pandit registration (backend only).


Requirements:
1. During Pandit registration, validate:
   - referral token
   - referral code
2. Ensure the referral:
   - exists
   - is active
   - has not exceeded its usage limit
3. Prevent registration if validation fails.
4. On successful validation:
   - increment referral usage count
   - create a referral usage record
   - set the new Pandit's referred_by field
5. Ensure referrer is not fully blocked.


Rules:
- Do NOT approve Pandits
- Do NOT auto-login users
- Do NOT create frontend templates
- Do NOT generate referral links
- Do NOT send notifications


Explain briefly:
- Why referral validation is strict
- Why referral does not imply approval


________________


📂 FILES EXPECTED (ONLY THESE)
   * accounts/views.py (extend registration logic)
   * referrals/services.py or helper logic (optional, backend-only)
❌ No new models
❌ No migrations
❌ No templates
________________


🧪 IMMEDIATE VERIFICATION CHECKLIST
Validation checks
   * Invalid token → ❌ blocked
   * Wrong code → ❌ blocked
   * Expired link → ❌ blocked
   * Usage limit reached → ❌ blocked
   * Valid referral → ✅ registration allowed
Data integrity
   * used_count increments correctly
   * ReferralUsage record created
   * referred_by set once
Safety
   * No approval logic triggered
   * No auto-login
   * No UI added
________________


⚠️ COMMON CURSOR FAILURES (WATCH CLOSELY)
❌ Auto-approves Pandit
❌ Skips usage limit
❌ Allows reuse beyond 5
❌ Adds notification logic
❌ Mixes dashboard logic
Any of these → REJECT
________________


🧠 WHY THIS TASK IS ISOLATED
Because:
   * Validation ≠ generation
   * Validation ≠ approval
   * Validation ≠ trust display
This separation prevents:
   * Abuse
   * Inconsistent data
   * Broken trust chains
________________

🔒 SECURITY & TRUST (COMMONLY IGNORED)
1️⃣ Soft Delete Everywhere (Very Important)
Problem: Developers delete rows → breaks audits & referrals.
Suggestion:
   * Add is_active / deleted_at instead of hard deletes
   * Especially for:
   * Pandits
   * Referrals
   * Reviews

🧠 Let me restate your requirement (to confirm understanding)
You want:
         1. Only Admin Pandits / Super Admins to:
         * Generate referral links
         * See “Generate Referral Link” button in their dashboard
         2. Referral link behavior:
         * Each generated link:
         * Allows ONLY 5 pandit registrations
         * Expires automatically after limit is reached (or time-based later)
         * Once expired:
         * Link becomes invalid
         * New link must be generated manually
         * Every new generation = new unique link + code
         3. Access control:
         * ❌ Normal pandits → CANNOT see or use generate-link feature
         * ❌ Newly registered pandits → NO admin role, NO referral access
         * ✅ Only:
         * Pandit with admin access
         * Super admin
         4. Registration flow:
         * Referral link opens Pandit Registration Page
         * No public pandit signup
         * Registration works ONLY via valid referral link
         5. Tracking & audit (VERY IMPORTANT):
         * Store:
         * Who generated the link
         * When it was generated
         * When it expires
         * How many times it was used
         * Who registered using that link
         * Who attempted to access it (optional)
         * All this data must be stored in a separate table
         * Referral link table must be linked to Pandit/User table
         6. Security:
         * Link should NOT be visible anywhere in frontend
         * No listing page
         * No sharing via UI
         * Only dashboard access for admins

________________


2️⃣ Data Design (Conceptual, no code)
You will have a separate table, for example:
ReferralInvite
         * id
         * generated_by (FK → User / Pandit)
         * referral_code
         * referral_link
         * max_uses = 5
         * current_uses
         * expires_at
         * is_active
         * created_at
ReferralUsage
         * id
         * referral_invite (FK)
         * registered_user (FK → User)
         * used_at
         * ip_address (optional)
This is exactly how professional SaaS invitation systems work.
________________


3️⃣ Role Rules (Very Clear)
User Type
	Can Generate Link
	Can Use Link
	Super Admin
	✅ Yes
	❌ No need
	Pandit Admin
	✅ Yes
	❌ No
	Approved Pandit
	❌ No
	✅ If invited
	New Pandit
	❌ No
	❌
	By default, newly registered pandit has ZERO referral or admin access.
________________


4️⃣ Dashboard Visibility (Frontend Rule)
         * “Generate Referral Link” button:
         * Visible ONLY if:
         * user.role == pandit_admin OR user.is_superadmin
         * Completely hidden for others
         * No JS-only hiding — backend enforced
________________


5️⃣ Link Expiry Logic (Backend Rule)
A referral link becomes invalid when:
         * current_uses >= max_uses
OR
         * expires_at < now
Invalid link:
            * Shows error page
            * Registration blocked
________________

            * No spam registrations

godaddy hosting-config
Good question — and your developer note is correct 👍
Earlier we said “NO storage configuration” inside modules, but hosting & deployment configuration is a separate concern, and it is allowed now.
I’ll answer this as a senior architect, clearly, without breaking any locked modules, and without touching code.
________________


✅ Hosting Django Project on GoDaddy — ARCHITECTURAL CONFIGURATION
This is INFRA / DEPLOYMENT, not application logic.
So it does NOT violate any earlier rules.
________________


🔴 FIRST: Important Reality Check (Very Important)
GoDaddy hosting types matter a LOT
GoDaddy offers:
                  1. ❌ Shared Hosting → NOT suitable for Django
                  2. ⚠️ VPS Hosting → OK for Django (recommended)
                  3. ✅ Dedicated Server → Best (costly)
👉 You MUST use VPS or Dedicated
If you only have shared hosting → Django will NOT work properly.
________________


✅ RECOMMENDED GO-DADDY SETUP (MINIMUM)
✔ Hosting Type
                  * GoDaddy VPS (Linux)
                  * OS: Ubuntu 20.04 or 22.04
                  * RAM: 2 GB minimum
                  * CPU: 1–2 cores
                  * Disk: 40+ GB
________________


🧩 REQUIRED STACK (STANDARD & SAFE)
1️⃣ Operating System
Ubuntu 20.04 LTS or 22.04 LTS


________________


2️⃣ Python Environment
Python 3.10 or 3.11
virtualenv or venv
pip


________________


3️⃣ Web Server (VERY IMPORTANT)
GoDaddy does NOT auto-configure Django.
You must install:
Component
	Purpose
	Nginx
	Web server (static + proxy)
	Gunicorn
	Django app server
	Supervisor
	Keep Gunicorn running
	________________


4️⃣ Database Choice (Your Decision)
You already decided:
✅ SQLite for now
So configuration is:
                  * SQLite file inside project directory
                  * File permissions properly set
                  * Daily backups (manual or cron later)
👉 No MySQL needed right now
________________


5️⃣ Domain & DNS (GoDaddy Panel)
In GoDaddy:
                  * Domain → DNS Management
                  * Add A Record
@ → VPS_PUBLIC_IP
www → VPS_PUBLIC_IP


________________


6️⃣ Static & Media Files (IMPORTANT)
Even with SQLite, Django needs this:
Type
	Location
	Static files
	/var/www/nityadharma/static/
	Media uploads
	/var/www/nityadharma/media/
	Handled by:
                  * Django collects static
                  * Nginx serves static/media
⚠️ No S3 / CDN required now (zero cost)
________________


7️⃣ Environment Variables (.env)
You MUST have:
DEBUG=False
SECRET_KEY=xxxxx
ALLOWED_HOSTS=yourdomain.com,www.yourdomain.com


SQLite config stays unchanged.
________________


🔐 SECURITY CONFIGURATION (MANDATORY)
Firewall
Allow: 22 (SSH)
Allow: 80 (HTTP)
Allow: 443 (HTTPS)
Block everything else


SSL (FREE)
                  * Use Let’s Encrypt
                  * Certbot with Nginx
________________


🧠 WHERE THIS FITS IN YOUR MODULE SYSTEM
Concern
	Module
	Hosting
	❌ Not in modules
	Nginx/Gunicorn
	❌ Not in modules
	SSL
	❌ Not in modules
	DB choice
	Module 0 (already done)
	Media rules
	Module 4
	Security enforcement
	Module 12
	👉 Hosting config is external infrastructure, so no module is broken.
________________


🧪 HOW YOU VERIFY IT WORKS (Browser-Level)
After deployment:
Open
https://yourdomain.com
                  1.                   2. Check:
                  * Homepage loads
                  * Static CSS loads
                  * Admin panel works
                  3. Upload:
                  * Pandit profile image
                  * Confirm it displays
________________


🚫 COMMON MISTAKES TO AVOID
❌ Trying Django on GoDaddy shared hosting
❌ Using Apache + mod_wsgi on GoDaddy (painful)
❌ Running Django with runserver
❌ Storing SQLite outside project folder
❌ Leaving DEBUG=True
________________


✅ FINAL ARCHITECT CONFIRMATION
                  * ✔ GoDaddy VPS is OK
                  * ✔ SQLite is OK for now
                  * ✔ No module rules broken
                  * ✔ No code changes required
                  * ✔ Can scale later to MySQL or RDS
                  * ✔ Zero extra cost tools

**Key Rules:**
- Approved master data: Official, used in search, SEO, trust display
- Suggested master data: Proposal only, must NOT appear in search, SEO, or trust
- Referral registration allowed but restricted (must use approved data)
- Suggested data completely isolated from public features
- No user blocking or penalties for submitting suggestions

## 📁 PROJECT STRUCTURE


```
Nitya Dharma/
├── accounts/                    # User & authentication app
│   ├── models.py               # Custom User model
│   ├── views.py                # Login, logout, registration, approval
│   ├── urls.py                 # Authentication URLs
│   ├── decorators.py           # Access control decorators
│   ├── utils.py                # Helper functions
│   └── migrations/             # User model migrations
├── core/                       # Core app (skeleton)
├── referrals/                  # Referral & trust chain app
│   ├── models.py               # ReferralLink, ReferralUsage models
│   ├── views.py                # Referral generation, deactivation
│   ├── urls.py                 # Referral URLs
│   └── migrations/             # Referral model migrations
├── nityadharma/                # Project package
│   ├── settings/               # Settings package
│   │   ├── base.py             # Common settings
│   │   ├── dev.py              # Development settings
│   │   └── prod.py             # Production settings
│   └── urls.py                 # Main URL configuration
├── templates/                  # Template files
│   └── base.html               # Base template
├── static/                     # Static files
├── media/                      # Media files
└── [Documentation Files]       # Strategy and contract documents
```

🧠 ARCHITECTURAL DECISIONS (FINAL & LOCKED)
1️⃣ Pandit is NOT a separate auth entity
                  * Pandit = User
                  * Identified by:
                  * user.role = pandit
                  * Uses the same Custom User model from Module 1
❌ No separate Pandit auth table
❌ No duplicate identity logic
________________


2️⃣ Pandit registration is NOT public
                  * ❌ No “Become a Pandit” public button
                  * ❌ No open signup
Pandit can register ONLY via:
                  * Valid referral link
                  * Valid referral code
                  * Generated by:
                  * Admin Pandit
                  * Super Admin
________________


3️⃣ Pandit onboarding lifecycle states
Pandit lifecycle is managed via user.status:
Status
	Meaning
	pending
	Registered via referral, awaiting approval
	active
	Approved Pandit (eligible for dashboard, profile, referrals if allowed)
	rejected
	Rejected by Admin / Super Admin
	🚫 No auto-approval
🚫 No extra states
🚫 No bypass
________________


4️⃣ Default values at registration
When a Pandit registers via referral:
                  * role = pandit
                  * status = pending
                  * Dashboard access → ❌ blocked
                  * Referral generation → ❌ blocked
                  * Profile visibility → ❌ blocked
                  * Search visibility → ❌ blocked
________________


5️⃣ Admin & Super Admin control points
Admin Pandit / Super Admin can:
                  * View pending Pandit registrations
                  * Approve Pandit → status becomes active
                  * Reject Pandit → status becomes rejected
Only after approval:
                  * Pandit becomes visible
                  * Pandit can access dashboard
                  * Pandit may gain referral privileges (Module 3 rules)
________________


6️⃣ Security principle applied
✔ NO FRONTEND-ONLY SECURITY
All checks must be:
                  * Backend-enforced
                  * Role-validated
                  * Status-validated
Frontend visibility is UX only, not security.
________________

                                                      * Has maximum 5 uses

                                                      * NOT used for permission inheritance

                                                      * OR creator becomes fully blocked

🧠 ELIGIBILITY RULES (FINAL & LOCKED)
1️⃣ Who CAN generate a referral link
A Pandit CAN generate a referral link only if all conditions below are met:
                                                      * Pandit role = pandit
                                                      * Pandit status = active
                                                      * Pandit is NOT fully blocked
                                                      * Pandit has admin access (admin pandit or super admin)
________________


2️⃣ Who CANNOT generate a referral link
A Pandit CANNOT generate a referral link if any of the following are true:
                                                      * Status ≠ active (pending / rejected)
                                                      * Fully blocked by Super Admin
                                                      * Limited blocked (temporarily restricted)
                                                      * Regular pandit without admin access
                                                      * Referral generation rate limit exceeded (policy-level)
________________


3️⃣ Behavior of existing referral links (VERY IMPORTANT)
If a referrer’s moderation state changes:
🔴 Referrer becomes fully blocked
                                                      * All active referral links:
                                                      * Are automatically marked inactive
                                                      * Cannot be used for new registrations
                                                      * Existing referral records:
                                                      * MUST remain intact
                                                      * MUST remain auditable
                                                      * MUST NOT be deleted
🟡 Referrer becomes limited blocked
                                                      * Referral link generation:
                                                      * ❌ Disabled temporarily
                                                      * Existing referral links:
                                                      * Remain active (unless explicitly disabled)
                                                      * Trust display handled later (Task 3.7)
________________


4️⃣ Audit preservation rule
                                                      * Referral links are never hard-deleted
                                                      * Deactivation is state-based, not destructive
                                                      * All historical referral data must remain available for:
                                                      * Disputes
                                                      * Compliance
                                                      * Trust chain verification
________________

🧠 RATE-LIMITING RULES (FINAL & LOCKED)
1️⃣ Referral Link Generation Limits
Referral link generation must be limited by:
                                                      * Pandit (admin)
                                                      * Time window
Policy example (final values can be tuned later):
                                                      * Max referral links per admin per day
                                                      * Max active referral links at a time
If limit is exceeded:
                                                      * ❌ New referral link generation is denied
                                                      * ✅ Existing links remain unaffected
                                                      * ❌ No auto-blocking occurs
________________


2️⃣ Referral Usage Attempt Limits
Referral usage (during registration) must be limited by:
                                                      * IP address
                                                      * Referral token
                                                      * Time window
Purpose:
                                                      * Prevent brute-force attempts
                                                      * Prevent referral code guessing
                                                      * Prevent automated abuse
If limit is exceeded:
                                                      * ❌ Registration attempt is blocked temporarily
                                                      * ❌ Referral link is NOT invalidated
                                                      * ❌ No moderation action is triggered
________________


3️⃣ Non-Paid Implementation Constraint
Rate-limiting must NOT require paid services.
Allowed implementation approaches (later):
                                                      * Django cache
                                                      * Database counters
                                                      * Timestamp comparisons
This task only locks the constraint, not the implementation.
________________

🧠 CORE PRINCIPLE (VERY IMPORTANT)
❗ There are NO default SEO values for Pandit profiles.
❗ SEO is always generated dynamically using placeholders.
❗ Placeholders resolve using current Pandit profile data.
This guarantees:
                                                      * Every Pandit has unique SEO
                                                      * SEO updates automatically when profile updates
                                                      * No two profiles compete with identical metadata
________________


🧠 PLACEHOLDER-BASED SEO CONTRACT (FINAL & LOCKED)
1️⃣ What “Placeholders” Mean
Placeholders are tokens, not static strings.
Examples:
                                                      * {pandit_name}
                                                      * {city}
                                                      * {local_area}
                                                      * {pooja_types}
                                                      * {experience_years}
                                                      * {referrer_name} (only if visible per Task 3.7)
                                                      * {trust_depth}
They are:
                                                      * Stored as templates
                                                      * Resolved at render time
                                                      * Always reflect current data
________________


2️⃣ Data That MAY Be Exposed (Read-Only, Placeholder-Only)
The Referral module may expose the following SEO-safe data points as placeholders:
                                                      * Referrer display name
                                                      * Referrer experience (if available)
                                                      * Trust chain depth (A → B → C)
                                                      * Referral relationship existence (boolean)
📌 These values:
                                                      * Are read-only
                                                      * Are never hardcoded
                                                      * Are never injected as defaults
________________


3️⃣ Data That MUST NOT Be Exposed
The Referral module must NEVER expose:
                                                      * Referral tokens
                                                      * Referral codes
                                                      * Internal IDs
                                                      * Referral usage counts
                                                      * Audit snapshot fields
                                                      * Moderation reasons
These are:
                                                      * Internal
                                                      * Security-sensitive
                                                      * Not SEO-safe
________________


4️⃣ Moderation-Aware Placeholder Rules
Placeholder availability must respect trust visibility rules (Task 3.7):
                                                      * Fully blocked referrer
                                                      * ❌ {referrer_name} placeholder not available
                                                      * Limited blocked referrer
                                                      * ⚠ {referrer_name} available with warning flag
                                                      * Active referrer
                                                      * ✅ Normal placeholder availability
No rendering logic here — only availability rules.
________________


5️⃣ Downstream Consumption (IMPORTANT)
This contract is consumed by:
                                                      * Module 4 — Pandit Profile
                                                      * SEO template storage
                                                      * Placeholder definitions
                                                      * Module 7 — Search & SEO Rendering
                                                      * Final meta tag resolution
                                                      * Structured data generation
📌 The Referral module:
                                                      * Exposes data
                                                      * Does NOT render SEO
________________

🧠 FINAL RULES (LOCKED & EXPLICIT)
________________


1️⃣ Approved vs Suggested Master Data
✅ Approved master data
Includes:
                                                      * Cities
                                                      * Local areas
                                                      * Poojas
Approved data:
                                                      * Is created/approved by Super Admin
                                                      * Appears in:
                                                      * Search filters
                                                      * SEO placeholder resolution
                                                      * Trust display
                                                      * Referral-based profiles
________________


❌ Suggested (unapproved) master data
                                                      * Exists only as a proposal
                                                      * Is NOT official
                                                      * Must NOT:
                                                      * Appear in search
                                                      * Be indexed
                                                      * Be used for SEO placeholders
                                                      * Affect trust chain display
________________


2️⃣ Referral-Based Registration Restrictions
When a Pandit registers via referral:
Allowed:
                                                      * Selecting approved master data
                                                      * Submitting suggestions via text input
Not allowed:
                                                      * Completing referral registration using only suggested data
                                                      * Having suggested data reflected in:
                                                      * SEO metadata (even as placeholders)
                                                      * Search results
                                                      * Trust chain display
📌 Referral registration itself remains valid, but:
                                                      * Trust, search, and SEO operate only on approved selections
________________


3️⃣ Trust Chain Protection Rule
                                                      * Trust chain relationships:
                                                      * Depend only on:
                                                      * Referral linkage
                                                      * Approved master data
                                                      * Are independent of suggestions
This prevents:
                                                      * Fake poojas inflating credibility
                                                      * Location spam via referrals
________________


4️⃣ SEO & Search Isolation Rule (UPDATED)
                                                      * Suggested master data:
                                                      * ❌ Must NOT generate SEO pages
                                                      * ❌ Must NOT appear in filters
                                                      * ❌ Must NOT be indexed
                                                      * ❌ Must NOT be exposed even as SEO placeholders
✔ SEO placeholders resolve ONLY from approved master data and active Pandit profile fields
Only after Super Admin approval does suggested data:
                                                      * Enter master tables
                                                      * Become selectable
                                                      * Become eligible for SEO placeholders and search
________________


5️⃣ No User Blocking or Penalty Side-Effects
                                                      * Submitting suggestions:
                                                      * ❌ Does NOT block the Pandit
                                                      * ❌ Does NOT invalidate referral
                                                      * ❌ Does NOT affect referral eligibility
                                                      * ❌ Does NOT affect profile visibility
Suggestion handling is non-punitive and advisory only.
________________

                                                      * Cursor AI cannot embed unsafe upload logic in the wrong module

🧩 MEDIA — CONCEPTUAL DEFINITION
Profile Media refers to visual or embedded content displayed on a Pandit’s public profile, intended only to:
                                                      * Improve trust
                                                      * Improve clarity
                                                      * Improve presentation
Media is decorative & supportive, never authoritative.
________________


📍 MEDIA TYPES (ALLOWED — DISPLAY ONLY)
The Pandit Profile may DISPLAY the following media types:
1️⃣ Profile picture (primary identity image)
2️⃣ Banner / cover image
3️⃣ Gallery images (limited set)
4️⃣ Embedded video links (e.g., YouTube URL)
📌 No media type is mandatory
📌 Absence of media must not block profile visibility
________________


🧠 MEDIA OWNERSHIP & CONTROL (VERY IMPORTANT)
🔹 What the Pandit Profile DOES
✅ Displays approved media
✅ Orders and groups media for presentation
✅ Respects visibility & moderation decisions
🔹 What the Pandit Profile NEVER DOES
❌ Upload files
❌ Store files
❌ Resize / compress images
❌ Validate file type or size
❌ Moderate content
❌ Decide approval or rejection
❌ Handle abuse or reports
The profile is a pure consumer.
________________


🧱 MEDIA RESPONSIBILITY SPLIT (LOCKED)
Concern
	Owner Module
	File upload
	Future Media Module
	Storage (local / S3 / CDN)
	Future Media Module
	File validation
	Future Media Module
	Virus / abuse checks
	Module 11 (Governance)
	Media approval / rejection
	Module 11
	Visibility enforcement
	Module 11
	Rendering media
	Module 7 (Search / Public Pages)
	Profile composition
	Module 4 (THIS MODULE)
	________________


🔒 HARD SAFETY RULES (LOCKED)
❌ Media must NEVER:
                                                      * Be stored directly in PanditProfile
                                                      * Bypass governance checks
                                                      * Be visible if Pandit is blocked
                                                      * Be visible if media is unapproved
                                                      * Be trusted for identity verification
                                                      * Affect trust score or ranking
                                                      * Influence search relevance directly
________________


🧠 MEDIA & VISIBILITY INTERACTION
This task locks the following truths:
                                                      * If a Pandit is limited-blocked or full-blocked,
→ media visibility is controlled entirely by Module 11
                                                      * The profile does not override or interpret these rules
                                                      * Media visibility is binary input to the profile:
                                                         * Show
                                                         * Hide
No logic lives here.
________________


🔗 EXPLICIT MODULE DEPENDENCIES
🔹 Profile Media DEPENDS ON:
                                                         * Future Media Module (uploads, storage, validation)
                                                         * Module 11 — Governance & Moderation
                                                         * Module 7 — Rendering rules
🔹 Profile Media is CONSUMED BY:
                                                         * Module 7 — Public profile rendering
                                                         * Module 10 — Trust display (visual only)
                                                         * Module 5 — Dashboards (editing UI later)

________________


🧩 DASHBOARD — CONCEPTUAL DEFINITION
A Dashboard is:
                                                            * A read-heavy control surface
                                                            * A signal aggregator
                                                            * A navigation hub to actions owned by other modules
A Dashboard is NOT:
                                                            * An authority
                                                            * A validator
                                                            * A rule engine
                                                            * A moderation system
                                                            * A source of truth
                                                            * An execution surface
                                                            * A governance workflow entry point
Dashboards reflect decisions — they never make them.
________________


🧠 ROLE-WISE DASHBOARD PURPOSE (LOCKED)
🔹 Pandit Dashboard
Purpose
                                                            * Manage own profile inputs
                                                            * View system signals, alerts, and readiness indicators
                                                            * Respond to requests initiated elsewhere (e.g., reviews, bookings later)
Boundaries
❌ Cannot approve anything
❌ Cannot moderate
❌ Cannot manage referrals
❌ Cannot view other Pandits’ private data
________________


🔹 Admin Pandit Dashboard
Purpose
                                                            * Perform delegated administrative observation
                                                            * View trust, referral, and report summaries
                                                            * Escalate issues to Super Admin
Boundaries
❌ Cannot override Super Admin decisions
❌ Cannot delete Pandits
❌ Cannot change system rules
❌ Cannot directly enforce actions
________________


🔹 Super Admin Dashboard
Purpose
                                                            * System-wide oversight
                                                            * Final governance visibility
                                                            * Access to all high-level signals and audits (summary only)
Boundaries
❌ No daily operational clutter
❌ No UI-based rule creation
❌ No direct enforcement via dashboard
________________


🔒 HARD BOUNDARIES (LOCKED)
Dashboards must NEVER:
❌ Enforce permissions
❌ Bypass moderation rules
❌ Mutate data owned by other modules
❌ Compute trust, ranking, or SEO
❌ Expose raw audit logs to Pandits
❌ Act as execution or governance engines
________________


🔗 EXPLICIT MODULE DEPENDENCIES

                                                            * Module 1 — Authentication & roles

                                                            * Human operators only (Pandits / Admins)
                                                            * ❌ No backend module depends on dashboards

🧩 AVAILABILITY — CONCEPTUAL DEFINITION (LOCKED)
Availability represents:
                                                            * Pandit-declared time windows
                                                            * An intent to be available
                                                            * A planning and discovery signal
Availability is:
                                                            * Calendar-based
                                                            * Day-wise & time-slot oriented
                                                            * Contextual (pooja, city, area)
                                                            * Editable by Pandit
                                                            * Read-only for consumers
Availability is NOT:
❌ A booking
❌ A reservation
❌ A guarantee
❌ A lead
❌ A conversion
❌ A payment trigger
________________


🧠 WHAT AVAILABILITY IS RESPONSIBLE FOR
Availability WILL:
1️⃣ Allow Pandits to define when they are available
2️⃣ Support day-wise and time-slot definitions
3️⃣ Support pooja-specific availability
4️⃣ Support city & local-area context
5️⃣ Emit read-only signals to:
                                                            * Search & filters
                                                            * Dashboards
                                                            * Recommendation systems (future)
6️⃣ Support conceptual auditability (change awareness)
________________


🚫 WHAT AVAILABILITY MUST NEVER DO
Availability MUST NEVER:
❌ Accept requests
❌ Confirm dates
❌ Lock time slots
❌ Reject users
❌ Handle payments
❌ Influence SEO or trust
❌ Act as a KPI or score
❌ Track or score leads
❌ Trigger notifications
________________

	Owner
	Availability definition
	Pandit
	Availability editing
	Pandit only
	Availability storage
	Module 6

	Other modules (read-only)

	❌ Not here
	Lead tracking
	❌ Not here
	________________

                                                               * Module 1 — Auth (Pandit identity)

                                                               * Module 4 — Pandit profile (context only)
🔹 Availability is CONSUMED BY:
                                                               * Module 5 — Dashboards (display only)
                                                               * Module 7 — Search & filters
                                                               * Recommendation engine (future, read-only)

5️⃣ Edit or remove availability entries at any time

❌ Accept bookings
❌ Confirm users
❌ See demand or requests
❌ Lock time slots

                                                               * Affect search ranking directly
